INDUSTRIAL FINANCE CORPORATION OF INDIA 
New Delhi, the 16th July 1987 
NOTICE 

No. 13/87.—NOTICE is hereby given that the Thirtyninth 
Annual General Meeting of the shareholders of the Indus¬ 
trial Finance Corporation of India will be held on Monday, 
the 28th Septemb^. 1987 at 4.00 P.M. in the ‘Banquet Hall’ 
of Hotel Kanishka, 19-Ashok Road, New Delhi-110001 to 
transact the following business :— 

(1) To read and consider the Balance Sheet and Profit 
& Loss Account of the Corporation, for the year 
ended the 30tb June, 1987, together with the 
Report by the Board on the working of the Cor¬ 
poration during the year, and the Auditors’ Report 
on the said Balance Sheet and Accounts. 

,(2) To elect under Section 34 of the Industrial Finance 
Corporation Act, 1948, one Auditor duly qualified 
to act as Auditor of Companies under sub-section 
(1) of Section 226 of the Companies Act, 1956 
(1 of 1956) by the shareholders mentioned in sub¬ 
section (3) of Section 4 of the Industrial Finance 
Corporation Act, 1948, namely scheduled banks, 
insurance companies, investment trusts aiid other 
like financial’ institutions, and co-operative banks, 
in place of M/s T. R, Chadha & Co., Chartered 
Accountants, New Delhi.
